### [105] [Thermal and viscous contrast in quantum Hall scanning-probe images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16315)
*P. Shubham Parashar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量子霍尔扫描成像的理论框架，通过Landau能级投影、Ward恒等式、磁化减除热电输运、Stokes-Ohm反演和有限针尖Fisher信息，将探针记录的有限分辨率输运算符泛函形式化。在强场尖锐Landau能级区，缺陷诱导的热电和电学霍尔对比度之比满足$δα_{xy}^{tr}/δσ_{xy}=(E_c-μ)/(eT)$，零对比度点由能量加权钉扎在$E_c=μ$而非缺陷形状。在流体动力学区，可测量的$q^2$张量振幅混合了多个通道，霍尔奇偶图像本身不能直接测量霍尔粘度。框架将量子霍尔纳米镜的视觉解读转化为电学、热电和粘性响应通道的定量可观测性测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔扫描图像通常被解读为局域势、温度或粘度的分布图，但实际上探针记录的是一个有限分辨率的输运算符泛函。这种直观解读与真实物理量之间的差距，导致无法准确提取材料内在的输运特性。本文旨在建立严格的数学框架，将成像数据与基础输运系数关联起来，并确定不同通道的可观测性。

Method: (1) Landau能级投影构建探针响应的量子力学基础；(2) 粒子数Ward恒等式确保电荷守恒约束；(3) 磁化减除热电输运消除热磁化电流的干扰；(4) 流体动力学Stokes-Ohm反演关联粘性与电输运；(5) 有限针尖Fisher信息量化测量精度。在两种互补输运体系中推导解析结果，并以石墨烯几何为例进行数值拟合。

Result: (1) 强场区：缺陷诱导的$δα_{xy}^{tr}/δσ_{xy}=(E_c-μ)/(eT)$，零对比度点仅取决于化学势与能级中心能量之差，与缺陷形状和针尖核无关。(2) 流体动力学区：$q^2$张量振幅混合多个输运通道，霍尔奇偶图像不能独立提取霍尔粘度。(3) 石墨烯示例：Schur补拟合对边界滑移等干扰参数的敏感性约为68平方纳米（SNR0=200），边界滑移是主要限制因素。

Conclusion: 该框架将量子霍尔纳米镜从视觉解读提升为定量可观测性测试工具，揭示了不同输运通道（电学、热电、粘性）的相互耦合关系，为实验设计提供了理论指导，特别是明确了哪种图像特征对应哪种物理机制。

Abstract: Quantum Hall scanning images are often read as maps of a local potential, temperature, or viscosity, whereas a probe records a finite-resolution functional of a transport operator. We formulate this functional using Landau-level projection, a particle-number Ward identity, magnetization-subtracted thermoelectric transport, a hydrodynamic Stokes-Ohm inversion, and finite-tip Fisher information. Two results follow in complementary transport regimes. In the strong-field, sharp-Landau-level regime, the defect-induced thermoelectric and electrical Hall contrasts of a smooth scalar defect obey $δα_{xy}^{tr}/δσ_{xy}=(E_c-μ)/(eT)$. At the retained long-wavelength order, the orbital form factor, defect geometry, and common tip kernel cancel after the heat-magnetization current is removed, so the zero of the thermoelectric contrast is pinned by energy weighting at $E_c=μ$ rather than by defect shape. In the hydrodynamic regime, the measurable $q^2$ tensor amplitudes mix Hall, longitudinal, transverse, boundary, electrothermal, and kinetic channels, so a Hall-odd image is not by itself a Hall-viscosity measurement. For a representative graphene geometry, a Schur-complement fit against the stated nuisance library yields a conditional one-standard-deviation sensitivity of approximately 68 square nanometers at SNR0 = 200, with boundary slip the limiting nuisance. The framework turns visual interpretation of quantum Hall nanoscopy into a quantitative observability test for electrical, thermoelectric, and viscous response channels.

</details>


### [106] [Observation of Critical Current Minimum in Super-Honeycomb Josephson Junction Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16500)
*Melissa Mikalsen,Alexander-Georg Penner,Samuel D. Escribano,Nadav Drechsler,Arunav Bordoloi,Jacob Issokson,Felix von Oppen,Yuval Oreg,Javad Shabani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种Al-InAs约瑟夫森结阵列（方形阵列、超蜂窝阵列及大间距超蜂窝控制器件），发现当相邻结间距小于安德烈夫束缚态相干长度时，在外加垂直磁场下临界电流在有理填充因子处出现峰值，反映稳定涡旋晶格构型；超蜂窝阵列中涡旋局域在不同非等同晶格位点；旋转面内磁场产生周期性临界电流振荡，归因于Rashba自旋轨道耦合；特别地，紧密排列的超蜂窝阵列在填充因子f=1时随面内磁场增大出现独特的临界电流最小值，该特征由超蜂窝涡旋晶格的独特几何结构与结间长程杂化共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超导-半导体约瑟夫森结阵列中局域安德烈夫束缚态在相邻结间距小于其相干长度时的杂化效应，以及不同晶格几何构型（方形与超蜂窝）对集体量子现象和涡旋行为的影响。

Method: 制备三种不同的Al-InAs约瑟夫森结阵列（方形阵列、超蜂窝阵列、大间距超蜂窝控制器件），在垂直磁场和旋转面内磁场下测量临界电流响应，结合受阻XY模型模拟进行对比分析。

Result: 垂直磁场下临界电流在有理填充因子处出现峰值；超蜂窝阵列中涡旋在不同填充因子下局域于非等同晶格位点；面内磁场旋转产生周期性临界电流振荡；仅在紧密排列的超蜂窝阵列中，f=1时观察到面内磁场增大导致的临界电流最小值。

Conclusion: 超蜂窝涡旋晶格的独特几何构型与结间长程杂化（ξ_ABS>d_JJ）共同导致了f=1时面内磁场下的临界电流最小值这一特征信号，揭示了晶格几何和束缚态杂化对约瑟夫森结阵列集体行为的协同调控作用。

Abstract: Superconductor-semiconductor Josephson junction arrays are a uniquely tunable platform for studying collective quantum phenomena, particularly in the regime where localized Andreev bound states can hybridize across the lattice when the physical separation between adjacent junctions is smaller than their coherence length ($ξ_{\text{ABS}}>d_{\text{JJ}}$). Here, we investigate three distinct Al-InAs Josephson junction arrays: a square array and super-honeycomb array fabricated within this ${ξ_{\text{ABS}}>d_{\text{JJ}}}$ regime, as well as a larger-spacing super-honeycomb control device designed such that $ξ_{\text{ABS}}\lesssim\!~d_{\text{JJ}}$. Under an out-of-plane field, critical current peaks emerge at rational filling factors, reflecting stable vortex configurations in the lattices. In the super-honeycomb lattice, vortices localize to distinct non-identical plaquettes at different filling factors, as predicted by frustrated XY model simulations. A rotating in-plane field yields periodic critical current oscillations that reflect the Rashba spin-orbit coupling inherent to the InAs quantum well. Surprisingly, at $f = 1$, the closely spaced super-honeycomb array exhibits a distinct critical current minimum as the magnitude of the in-plane field increases, a signature absent in the square array and large-spacing super-honeycomb array. These results indicate that this signature is jointly influenced by the unique geometry of the super-honeycomb vortex lattice and by long-range inter-junction hybridization.

</details>


### [107] [Topological magnon noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16574)
*Shuang Liang,Tengyue Zhao,Yu-Hang Li,Hua Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了铁磁绝缘体中由温度梯度驱动的磁子输运的完整形式化体系，推导了基于拓扑磁子绝缘体自旋哈密顿量的磁子流和磁子噪声公式，发现吉尔伯特阻尼导致磁子流不守恒，从而深刻改变了局部与非局部噪声间的互易关系、电导与热噪声间的Johnson-Nyquist公式以及透射系数与散粒噪声间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立温度梯度驱动的铁磁绝缘体中磁子输运的完整理论框架，特别是考虑拓扑磁子绝缘体的自旋哈密顿量，以准确描述磁子霍尔效应及噪声特性。

Method: 基于拓扑磁子绝缘体的自旋哈密顿量，推导了磁子流和磁子噪声的公式，计算了磁子霍尔角，给出了Fano因子的显式表达式，并重新确认了磁子电导与磁子噪声之间的定量关系。

Result: 发现吉尔伯特阻尼导致磁子流不守恒；局部与非局部噪声间的互易关系、Johnson-Nyquist公式以及透射系数与散粒噪声的关系均被深刻改变。

Conclusion: 吉尔伯特阻尼的存在从根本上改变了磁子输运中的噪声特性与标准关系，需重新审视磁子系统中的涨落-耗散定理和输运关系。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive formalism for magnon transport in ferromagnetic insulators driven by a temperature gradient. The formulas for magnon current and corresponding magnon noise are derived herein based on the spin Hamiltonian of a topological magnon insulator, which enables us to calculate the magnon Hall angle, to provide an explicit expression for the Fano factor, and to reaffirm the quantitative relations between magnon conductance and magnon noise. We find that the magnon current is not conserved in the presence of the Gilbert damping. Consequently, the reciprocal relation between the local and nonlocal noises, the Johnson-Nyquist formula between the conductance and the thermal noise, and the relation between the transmission coefficient and the shot noise are profoundly altered.

</details>


### [108] [Distributed Topological Charge and Spinorial Holonomy in Yukawa-Regularized Graphene Disclinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16835)
*A. M. de M. Carvalho,G. Q. Garcia,E. Brito,C. Furtado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Yukawa型正则化方法，将锥形几何中由向错导致的奇异曲率替换为平滑分布，并解析得到了共形因子、曲率和拓扑荷的精确表达式。该几何呈现尺度依赖的拓扑荷和半径依赖的完整相位，建立了分布曲率与几何相位之间的直接联系。进一步研究了无质量狄拉克准粒子在该背景下的动力学，发现正则化核心在保持缺陷拓扑特征的同时修正了自旋联络。这项工作为传统锥形描述提供了有限核心扩展，为石墨烯类系统中的几何与拓扑效应研究提供了自然框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥形几何是描述向错的标准工具，但其曲率集中在奇异顶点，这在实际物理系统中（如石墨烯）并不现实。作者希望引入一种平滑的正则化方法，在保持缺陷渐近拓扑结构的同时，消除曲率奇异性，从而更真实地描述实际材料中的几何效应。

Method: 采用Yukawa型势函数对锥形几何的曲率奇点进行正则化，通过解析求解得到平滑后的共形因子、曲率分布以及封闭拓扑荷的精确表达式。随后将这一几何背景应用于无质量狄拉克准粒子的动力学分析，研究正则化核心对自旋联络和拓扑签名的影响。

Result: 得到了正则化后几何的精确解析表达式；发现了尺度依赖的拓扑荷和半径依赖的完整相位（holonomy），建立了分布曲率与几何相位之间的直接联系；证明了正则化核心在保持渐近拓扑特征的前提下修正了自旋联络。

Conclusion: 该工作为锥形向错描述提供了有限核心的正则化扩展，消除了曲率奇异性，同时保留了拓扑缺陷的渐近特征。这一框架为研究石墨烯等二维材料中的几何与拓扑效应提供了更真实的理论工具。

Abstract: Conical geometries provide the standard description of disclinations, but they concentrate the curvature at a singular apex. We introduce a Yukawa-type regularization that replaces this singularity by a smooth curvature distribution while preserving the asymptotic topology of the defect. Exact expressions are obtained for the conformal factor, curvature, and enclosed topological charge. The resulting geometry exhibits a scale-dependent topological charge and a corresponding radius-dependent holonomy, establishing a direct connection between distributed curvature and geometric phases. We further investigate the dynamics of massless Dirac quasiparticles in this background and show that the regularized core modifies the spin connection while preserving the asymptotic topological signature of the defect. These results provide a finite-core extension of the conventional conical description and offer a natural framework for studying geometric and topological effects in graphene-like systems.

</details>


### [109] [Vacancy-Induced Topological Phase Transition via Valley Annihilation in an Anisotropic Honeycomb Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16965)
*Anna Hassine,Amit Goft,Boris Rotstein,Eric Akkermans*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现，在参数空间本征拓扑平庸的二维蜂窝晶格中，单个缺失原子（空位）即可驱动拓扑相变。空位作为内部边界，其缺陷绕数ν₃在t'/t<2时为∓1，在t'/t=2时由于狄拉克谷合并湮灭导致有效赝自旋自由度从m=2降至m=1，绕数突变为ν₃=0——这是在固定对称性类(BDI)内、由体Lifshitz相变驱动且仅通过空位可观测的拓扑相变。零模空间局域化程度在临界点达到极值，局域态密度中的波前位错可直接成像ν₃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在固定对称性类内，仅通过单个点缺陷（非周期性调制或全局参数改变）能否驱动拓扑相变，打破体拓扑平庸性并实现可观测的拓扑不变量突变。

Method: 在具有各向异性最近邻跃迁比t'/t的二维蜂窝晶格中引入单个空位，利用缺陷绕数ν₃和Nielsen-Ninomiya定理分析拓扑性质，通过逆参与比(IPR)和局域态密度波前位错表征相变。

Result: 单个空位在t'/t<2时具有非零缺陷绕数ν₃=∓1；在t'/t=2时因狄拉克谷合并、有效自由度降至m=1（破坏d+D+1=2m条件），绕数突变为ν₃=0；零模从代数局域(1/r)转变为更强约束，IPR在临界点达到尖锐最小值。

Conclusion: 首次在固定对称性类内实现由单点缺陷驱动的拓扑相变，该相变本质由体Lifshitz相变导致，通过局域态密度波前位错可直接成像缺陷绕数，在石墨烯、光子晶格和冷原子体系可实验验证。

Abstract: A single missing atom can drive a topological phase transition in a lattice that is otherwise trivial for all values of its parameters. We demonstrate this in a two-dimensional honeycomb lattice with anisotropic nearest-neighbor hopping ratio $t'/t$. The pristine lattice is topologically trivial for all $t'/t$ by the Nielsen-Ninomiya fermion-doubling theorem: Dirac valleys appear in pairs whose topological charges cancel identically in any bulk invariant. A single vacancy breaks this cancelation, acting as an internal boundary with defect winding number $ν_3=\mp 1$ for $t'/t<2$. At $t'/t=2$, the two Dirac valleys merge and annihilate; the number of active pseudospinor degrees of freedom drops from $m=2$ to $m=1$, violating the condition $d+D+1=2m$ required for a non-trivial winding number. The winding number collapses to $ν_3=0$: a topological phase transition within a fixed symmetry class (BDI), driven entirely by a bulk Lifshitz transition and observable only through the vacancy. The defect zero mode crosses over from algebraic (${\sim}1/r$) to stronger spatial confinement, with its inverse participation ratio reaching a sharp minimum at criticality. Wavefront dislocations in the local density of states provide a direct, spatially resolved image of $ν_3$, accessible in graphene and in photonic and cold-atom analogs.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum-metric-driven light-induced ferrovalley state in d-wave altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17049)
*Shihao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出d波交变磁体作为理想平台，利用线偏振非共振光驱动纯粹由量子度量介导的轨道选择性铁谷相变，实现了从贝里曲率中分离量子度量，并通过谷隙差异提供量子度量的直接定量测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度量与贝里曲率在量子材料中几乎总是共存，难以分离各自的贡献，这是当前量子材料研究的一个核心挑战。

Method: 利用Magnus展开和精确Floquet对角化，研究线偏振非共振光在d波交变磁体中的驱动效应。

Result: 发现光驱动导致纯粹的量子度量介导的轨道选择性铁谷相变，贝里曲率在任何阶数均无贡献；谷隙差异可解析地用量子度量表达。

Conclusion: d波交变磁体提供了一个纯净、可调谐的平台，其中量子度量效应可以被隔离、通过光偏振控制，并通过谷极化读取。

Abstract: Isolating the quantum metric from the Berry curvature remains a central challenge in quantum materials, as the two geometric quantities nearly always coexist and their contributions are difficult to disentangle. We show that d-wave altermagnets, whose real Hamiltonian possesses strictly vanishing Berry curvature, constitute an ideal platform for overcoming this obstacle. Using the Magnus expansion and exact Floquet diagonalization, we demonstrate that linearly polarized off-resonant light drives an orbital-selective ferrovalley phase through a purely quantum-metric--mediated band-gap renormalization, with no Berry curvature contribution at any order. The orbital selectivity originates from the hopping anisotropy, which generates a pronounced metric anisotropy between the $d_{xz}$ and $d_{yz}$ orbitals, and the gap reduction is expressed analytically in terms of the quantum metric. The resulting valley gap difference provides a direct, quantitative measure of the quantum metric, accessible to spin-resolved ARPES and optical pump-probe spectroscopy. This establishes d-wave altermagnets as a pristine, tunable platform in which quantum metric effects can be isolated, controlled by light polarization, and read out through valley polarization.

</details>


### [111] [Geometry contribution to sound attenuation in double-Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17064)
*Varsha Subramanyan,Shi-Zeng Lin,Avadh Saxena*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 双Weyl半金属中，应变耦合表现为对称性破缺的指向场，导致费米面几何变形，从而产生独特的声衰减机制，而这种几何贡献在简单Weyl半金属中完全不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双Weyl半金属中应变与声衰减的耦合机制，特别是与简单Weyl半金属中轴向耦合的差异。

Method: 理论分析双Weyl半金属中应变作为对称性破缺指向场对费米面的变形作用，并以此推导声衰减的几何贡献。

Result: 发现双Weyl半金属中缺乏轴向耦合，费米面几何变形是声衰减的唯一来源，识别出简单Weyl半金属中完全不存在的一种几何贡献机制。

Conclusion: 双Weyl半金属中的声衰减源于费米面几何变形这一独特机制，与简单Weyl半金属的轴向耦合机制有本质区别。

Abstract: The axial coupling of strain to the nodes of the simple Weyl semimetals leads to anomalous contributions to sound attenuation in such materials. However, in double Weyl semimetals, there is no such axial coupling. Strain instead couples as a symmetry-breaking director field that deforms the Fermi surface around each Weyl node. In this work, we show that absence of axial coupling in double Weyl semimetals implies a very different mechanism of relaxation due to sound. The deformed geometry of the Fermi surface is the only source of sound attenuation under these conditions. Thus, we identify a geometric contribution to sound attenuation in double Weyl semimetals that is entirely absent in simple Weyl semimetals.

</details>


### [112] [Spin-valley-layer coupling with dual control via stacking and electric field in antiferromagnetic bilayer Janus YIBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17199)
*Bo-Wen Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算和有效低能模型，研究了六种不同堆叠方式的双层Janus YIBr反铁磁二维半导体结构。结果表明，磁各向异性方向随堆叠方式变化，价带中存在至少0.3 eV的狄拉克相对论色散关系，并实现了自旋、谷和层的耦合，包括自旋劈裂、谷极化和量子谷霍尔绝缘体。外加电场可有效调控费米能级附近空穴的自旋、谷和层属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁二维半导体的改性和增强被认为是实现新型电子特性和促进应用的关键，因此需要深入探索其物理机制和调控手段。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算和有效低能模型，研究了六种不同堆叠方式的二维双层Janus YIBr反铁磁结构，计算了磁各向异性能、能带结构、自旋和原子属性以及贝里曲率。

Result: 磁各向异性方向随堆叠变化；价带存在至少0.3 eV宽能量窗口的狄拉克相对论色散；实现了自旋-谷-层耦合，包括自旋劈裂、谷极化和量子谷霍尔绝缘体；外加电场可调控空穴的自旋、谷和层属性。

Conclusion: 双层Janus YIBr结构具有丰富的自旋、谷和层耦合特性，外加电场可实现有效调控，为未来探索新型量子特性和多功能器件提供了基础。

Abstract: The modification and enhancement of antiferromagnetic two-dimensional semiconductor is considered crucial for realizing novel electronic properties and facilitating promising applications. For this purpose, we investigate six antiferromagnetic 2D bilayer Janus YIBr structures with different stacking variations by means of first-principles calculation and an effective low-energy model. The calculation of magnetic anisotropy energy shows that the direction of easy axis varies with different stacking. First-principles-calculated energy bands reveal that there is a Dirac relativistic dispersion relation in the valence band in a wide energy window of 0.3 eV at least. The calculations for spin, atom properties and Berry curvature description show that there is spin, valley and layer coupling with spin splitting, valley polarization and quantum valley Hall insulators can be achieved in the bilayer Janus structures. Further analyses of the effect of external electric field can be used to control spin, valley and layer of the hole near the Fermi level. These can be useful in future exploration for novel properties, control methods and more functionalities in bilayer Janus structures.

</details>


### [113] [Layer Edelstein Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17209)
*Binchang Zhou,Pan Zhou,Baoru Pan,Yuzhong Hu,Songmin Liu,Lizhong Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“层Edelstein效应”（LEE）的双层体系通用电流诱导自旋现象，定义为在面内电荷电流驱动下，顶层和底层出现方向相反的层分辨自旋磁化，且可通过外电场调控。通过最小双层k·p理论建立其普遍存在性，结合对称性分析和双层堆叠框架推导出模型无关的对称性判据，证明LEE在广泛非磁双层堆叠体系中普遍允许。LEE具有两种普适表现形式：对称性直接要求的显式层反向自旋磁化分量，以及外电场对称性约化下激活的分量。对堆叠双层MoSSe、MoTe₂和WTe₂的第一性原理计算验证了该效应及其实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索电流控制磁性的通用机制，为下一代自旋电子学功能提供基础物理原理和统一理论框架。

Method: 采用最小双层k·p理论模型建立LEE的普遍存在性；通过对称性分析和通用双层堆叠框架推导模型无关的对称性判据；利用第一性原理计算在MoSSe、MoTe₂、WTe₂等实际材料中进行验证。

Result: 证明了LEE在非磁双层堆叠体系中普遍存在；揭示了两种通用表现形式；第一性原理计算确认了该效应并展示了实验可行性。

Conclusion: LEE是双层体系中受对称性调控的通用响应现象，为电学产生和操控层分辨自旋极化提供了统一的概念框架。

Abstract: Electrical control of magnetism represents a fundamental route toward next-generation spintronic functionalities. In this Letter, we introduce a universal current-induced spin phenomenon in bilayer systems, termed the layer Edelstein effect (LEE), which serves as the natural counterpart of the layer Hall effect in real space. It is defined by the emergence of layer-resolved spin magnetizations with opposite components on the top and bottom layers, driven by an in-plane charge current and controllable by an external electric field. We establish the general existence of the LEE using a minimal bilayer $k \cdot p$ theory. By combining symmetry analysis with a general bilayer stacking framework, we derive a model-independent symmetry criterion demonstrating that the LEE is generically allowed in a broad class of nonmagnetic bilayer stacking systems. We further show that the LEE admits two universal manifestations: explicit layer-opposite spin magnetization components mandated directly by symmetry, and components become activated upon symmetry reduction by external electric fields. First-principles calculations on stacked bilayer MoSSe, MoTe$_2$ and WTe$_2$ confirm the predicted effect and illustrate their experimental feasibility. Our work establishes the LEE as a generic symmetry-governed response of bilayer systems, providing a unified conceptual framework for electrically generating and manipulating layer-resolved spin polarization.

</details>


### [114] [Colored $Δ_T$ noise probes the topological character of edge modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17354)
*Sachiraj Mishra,Colin Benjamin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了有色Δ_T噪声（有限频率Δ_T噪声）作为量子霍尔和量子自旋霍尔系统中边缘模式（EM）输运的探测手段。通过分析不同边缘模式（手性、自旋守恒螺旋、自旋翻转螺旋）在零平均电荷电流条件下的有限频率涨落特征，发现有色Δ_T噪声具有频率依赖的符号反转，能够有效区分不同边缘模式，并避免焦耳热干扰。研究还表明电子-空穴不对称性会显著改变频谱但保留区分特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的直流电导和噪声测量难以揭示有限频率非平衡电流涨落和动力学输运特性，且受焦耳热干扰。需要一种能够在零平均电荷电流条件下直接探测本征热涨落的方法，以区分量子霍尔和量子自旋霍尔系统中不同的边缘模式输运机制。

Method: 理论分析有色Δ_T噪声（有限频率噪声），在温度偏置和电压偏置下、零平均电荷电流条件下研究不同边缘模式（手性、自旋守恒螺旋、自旋翻转螺旋）的有限频率噪声谱。引入量子点接触中的能量依赖散射，分析电子-空穴不对称性对谱的影响。同时对比零温度有色量子散粒噪声。

Result: 不同边缘模式（手性、自旋守恒螺旋、自旋翻转螺旋）在有Δ_T噪声下表现出不同的频率特征；有色Δ_T噪声存在频率依赖的符号反转，而白噪声（ω=0）无此特征；电子-空穴不对称性显著改变频谱但保留区分特征；零温度有色量子散粒噪声中，手性模式消失，自旋守恒螺旋响应随频率变号，自旋翻转螺旋（平凡）呈正谱，但有色Δ_T噪声仍保持符号反转特征。

Conclusion: 有色Δ_T噪声可作为识别手性、自旋守恒螺旋和自旋翻转螺旋（平凡）边缘模式输运的鲁棒、实验可行的互补探测手段。

Abstract: We investigate colored $Δ_T$ noise, i.e., finite-frequency $Δ_T$ noise, as a probe of edge-mode (EM) transport in quantum Hall and quantum spin Hall systems. Colored $Δ_T$ noise probes finite-frequency nonequilibrium current fluctuations and dynamical transport properties that are often obscured in DC measurements of conductance and noise. Since $Δ_T$ noise is driven solely by a temperature and voltage bias under zero average charge current conditions, it eliminates current-induced Joule heating and directly probes intrinsic thermal fluctuations. We show that chiral, spin-conserving helical, and spin-flip helical (trivial) EMs exhibit distinct colored $Δ_T$-noise signatures under appropriate bias protocols. Incorporating energy-dependent scattering through a quantum point contact, we demonstrate that electron-hole asymmetry significantly modifies the finite-frequency spectrum while preserving these distinguishing features. Notably, colored $Δ_T$ noise exhibits a frequency-dependent sign reversal absent in the corresponding white ($ω=0$) $Δ_T$ noise. We further investigate zero-temperature colored quantum shot noise and find that it vanishes identically for chiral EMs, whereas the spin-conserving helical response changes sign with frequency. By contrast, spin-flip helical (trivial) EMs exhibit a positive colored shot-noise spectrum. However, the corresponding colored $Δ_T$ noise retains its characteristic sign reversal, providing a robust distinction between spin-conserving helical and spin-flip helical (trivial) EM transport. These results establish colored $Δ_T$ noise as a robust, experimentally accessible, complementary probe for identifying chiral, spin-conserving helical, and spin-flip helical (trivial) EM transport in mesoscopic topological systems.

</details>


### [115] [A field theory approach to Breit-type Hamiltonians in gapped Dirac systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17361)
*Xinhong Zhou,T. H. Hansson,Varsha Subramanyan,Qing-Dong Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种基于路径积分的场论方法，用于推导带隙Dirac系统在矢量和轴矢量规范场耦合下的低能Breit型哈密顿量，发现轴矢量场会引入传统电磁情形中不存在的矢量-轴矢量耦合项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理Dirac系统与轴矢量规范场耦合的低能有效哈密顿量时存在局限，传统Breit哈密顿量仅适用于普通电磁（矢量）情形，无法描述具有轴矢量场的系统（如Weyl半金属中的动力学轴场）。

Method: 以质量隙为大能量尺度，通过路径积分场论将Dirac旋量的高能部分积掉，保留低能自由度的正则Schrödinger描述，从而逐阶导出Breit型哈密顿量。

Result: 对于普通Dirac方程，该方法逐阶复现了传统Breit哈密顿量；当存在轴矢量规范场时，哈密顿量中出现了额外的矢量-轴矢量耦合项，这是传统电磁情形所没有的。作者用三维和二维Dirac模型进行了演示。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至有隙Weyl系统，其中动力学轴矢量场能产生独特的低能输运特征，为研究拓扑材料中的轴反常及相关输运现象提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a path-integral-based field theory for deriving Breit-type low-energy Hamiltonians for gapped Dirac systems coupled to both vector and axial gauge fields. Treating the mass gap as the large energy scale, we integrate out the high-energy component of the Dirac spinor and obtain a canonical Schrödinger description for the remaining low-energy degrees of freedom. For the ordinary Dirac equation, our method reproduces the conventional Breit Hamiltonian order by order. In the presence of axial gauge fields, however, the resulting Hamiltonian contains additional vector-axial couplings that have no analogue in the traditional electromagnetic case. We illustrate the method using three- and two-dimensional Dirac models and discuss its relevance to gapped Weyl systems, where dynamical axial fields can generate distinctive low-energy transport signatures.

</details>


### [116] [Resonant excitations via low frequency pumping in driven magnon systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18073)
*Jan Mathis Giesen,Alexandre Abbass Hamadeh,Imke Schneider,Philipp Pirro,Sebastian Eggert*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用Floquet理论分析了铁磁磁子在微波泵浦下的共振激发，重点关注驱动频率低于对应磁子能量的情况，通过参数共振在大参数区域内实现激发。研究建立了可解析预测共振区域和共振阈值的理论框架，针对YIG等铁磁/亚铁磁薄膜，分析了阻尼、振幅和频率的影响，并与微磁模拟结果进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用低于磁子能量的低频驱动来激发特定波数磁子的可能性，从而克服传统高频驱动的限制。

Method: 采用Floquet理论分析参数共振，推导共振区域和阈值的解析表达式；通过准能量的例外点（exceptional points）划分共振区域；利用微磁学模拟进行验证。

Result: 共振区域由准能量的例外点分隔；阈值振幅与阻尼之间存在特征幂律关系；实现了利用低频驱动对选定波数磁子的定向激发。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为利用低频微波泵浦在铁磁薄膜中定向激发特定波数磁子提供了可行方案，具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: We analyse resonant excitations of ferromagnetic magnons via microwave pumping using Floquet theory. Special focus is put on driving frequencies that are below the corresponding magnon energy, which can be excited in large parameter regions via parametric resonances. We develop a theoretical framework that analytically predicts the regions of resonances and resonance thresholds in thin films of ferro- and ferri-magnetic materials like YIG as a function of damping, amplitude and frequency. Resonance regions are separated by exceptional points of the quasi-energies and the results are compared with micromagnetic simulations. The corresponding threshold amplitudes can be estimated from a characteristic powerlaw with damping, leading to the possibility of targeted exciatations at selected wavenumbers using low frequency drive.

</details>


### [117] [Symmetry-isolated magnetoelectric electro-optic effects in noncentrosymmetric metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17392)
*C. O. Ascencio,D. J. P. de Sousa,Seungjun Lee,Tony Low*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文分类了时间反演对称非中心对称金属中Berry曲率偶极子D、陀磁张量K和磁电电光张量G的对称性约束形式，识别出11个空间群中D和K对称性消失而G仍然允许，为观测磁电电光效应提供了更直接途径。第一性原理计算验证了代表性材料中D和K为零而G可调，并提出了区分D和G驱动电光信号的实验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非中心对称金属中，Berry曲率偶极子D、陀磁张量K和磁电电光张量G驱动的光学和电光效应可能相互叠加，难以区分。作者希望通过对称性分析识别出D和K被对称性禁止而G仍然允许的空间群，从而更直接地观测与G相关的磁电电光效应，并设计实验方案区分不同机制的贡献。

Method: 通过群论分类分析时间反演对称非中心对称金属中D、K和G三者的对称性约束形式，识别出11个D和K对称性消失而G允许的空间群。利用第一性原理计算验证代表性材料中张量分量的存在性，并分析费米能级调控对G的影响。进一步通过斜入射几何中的光学响应分析，区分D和G驱动的电光信号：D驱动产生螺旋度偶的吸收/增益修正，G驱动产生s-p光学耦合和偏置诱导的圆二色性，具有sinθcosθ角依赖特征。

Result: 识别出11个空间群中D和K对称性为零而G仍然允许；第一性原理计算验证了代表性材料的零张量性质；发现在D和K对称性允许的情况下也可通过实验配置区分信号；斜入射几何中D驱动响应为螺旋度偶吸收/增益修正，G驱动响应产生s-p耦合和偏置诱导圆二色性，呈现sinθcosθ角依赖。

Conclusion: 对称性约束分析有效分离了D、K和G在非中心对称金属中的贡献，识别的11个空间群为观测纯G驱动的磁电电光效应提供了材料选择指导。提出的斜入射几何实验方案可有效区分D和G驱动的电光信号，为实验探测磁电电光效应提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We classify the symmetry-constrained forms of the Berry curvature dipole $\mathbf{D}$, gyrotropic magnetic tensor $\mathbf{K}$, and magnetoelectric electro-optic (EO) tensor $\mathbf{G}$, which describe metallic optical and EO effects in time-reversal symmetric, noncentrosymmetric metals. We identify 11 space groups (SGs) in which $\mathbf{D}$ and $\mathbf{K}$ vanish by symmetry while $\mathbf{G}$ remains allowed, thereby providing a more direct route to observing the recently predicted magnetoelectric EO effects associated with $\mathbf{G}$. First-principles based calculations confirm that $\mathbf{D}$ and $\mathbf{K}$ vanish for representative materials, while $\mathbf{G}$ remains allowed and tunable via Fermi level shifting. We further show that the choices of SG and experimental configuration provide complementary paths for isolating $\mathbf{G}$-driven EO effects, including cases where $\mathbf{D}$ and $\mathbf{K}$ are also symmetry-allowed. In an oblique-incidence geometry, the $\mathbf{D}$-driven response produces a helicity-even absorption or gain correction, whereas the $\mathbf{G}$-driven response couples the $s$ and $p$ optical sectors and produces a bias-induced circular dichroism with a characteristic $\sinθ\cosθ$ angular dependence. This provides a direct experimental route for separating the $\mathbf{D}$- and $\mathbf{G}$-driven EO signatures in noncentrosymmetric metals.

</details>


### [118] [Tilted Anisotropic Dirac Fermions in an Exponentially Decaying Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17676)
*Sushmita Saha,Alestin Mawrie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种解析理论，用于描述倾斜各向异性狄拉克费米子在指数衰减磁场中的朗道量子化。通过各向异性缩放和洛伦兹变换，将实验室坐标系问题转化为 boosted 坐标系中的各向同性狄拉克方程，从而获得精确解。反变换回实验室坐标系后，得到包含能量依赖引导中心参数的隐式量子化条件，并确定了物理允许态的条件。该形式在均匀场极限下恢复了已知结果，建立了描述倾斜各向异性狄拉克材料中指数衰减磁场下朗道量子化的统一洛伦兹协变框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要针对均匀磁场下的朗道量子化，而实际材料中可能面临空间非均匀（如指数衰减）磁场；倾斜各向异性狄拉克费米子缺乏统一的解析处理框架。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用各向异性缩放将各向异性问题映射为各向同性形式，再通过洛伦兹变换将倾斜狄拉克锥问题转化为 boosted 坐标系中的标准狄拉克方程；在 boosted 坐标系中求解指数衰减磁场问题，再通过逆变换回实验室坐标系得到隐式量子化条件。

Result: 得到了具有能量依赖引导中心参数的隐式朗道量子化条件；明确了物理上允许的量子化态的选择条件；在指数衰减磁场趋近均匀场的极限下，理论自然恢复了已知的倾斜各向异性狄拉克费米子的均匀场朗道能谱。

Conclusion: 该工作为处理倾斜各向异性狄拉克材料在非均匀（指数衰减）磁场中的朗道量子化提供了统一的洛伦兹协变解析理论，拓展了朗道量子化理论的应用范围。

Abstract: We develop an analytical theory of Landau quantization for tilted anisotropic Dirac fermions in an exponentially decaying magnetic field. Using anisotropy scaling and a Lorentz transformation, we recast the laboratory-frame problem into the isotropic Dirac equation in the boosted frame, where the exponentially decaying magnetic-field problem admits an exact solution. Transforming the boosted-frame spectrum back to the laboratory frame yields an implicit quantization condition with an intrinsically energy-dependent guiding-center parameter. We identify the conditions for physically admissible states. We further show that the formalism recovers the known uniform-field spectrum of tilted anisotropic Dirac fermions in the appropriate limit. Our results establish a unified Lorentz-covariant framework for describing Landau quantization in tilted anisotropic Dirac materials in the presence of an exponentially decaying magnetic field.

</details>


### [119] [Depth Determination of Individual Shallow NV-Centers via Spin-Lock NMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17734)
*Aaron Daniel,Beat Bürgler,Patrick Maletinsky,Patrick Potts*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出Spin-Lock NMR作为一种替代传统XY8脉冲序列的方法，用于精确测定金刚石中浅层NV色心的深度。通过调节Spin-Lock Rabi频率至1H拉莫尔频率，利用Hartmann-Hahn共振探测1H NMR信号，避免了脉冲序列的谐波模糊，并具有更高的光谱分辨率。该方法与XY8协议结果高度一致，并可用于研究金刚石表面本征的1H核自旋信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统XY8脉冲序列由于有限脉宽会产生次谐波响应，引入13C核自旋的不期望贡献，且仪器受限的光谱分辨率无法充分采样窄的1H NMR线形，需要一种更准确、无谐波模糊的NV深度测定方法。

Method: 利用Spin-Lock NMR技术，将Spin-Lock Rabi频率调谐到1H拉莫尔频率，通过Hartmann-Hahn共振探测1H NMR信号；从马尔可夫主方程推导出定量拟合函数，直接关联测量谱与NV深度。

Result: 该方法得到的NV深度估计值与已建立的XY8协议在多个NV色心上高度一致；成功应用于研究金刚石表面在无浸油情况下本征存在的1H核自旋信号。

Conclusion: Spin-Lock NMR是一种稳健的定量单NV深度测定替代方案，避免了脉冲序列的谐波模糊问题，具有更高的光谱分辨率，并扩展了NV色心深度测量的应用范围。

Abstract: Quantitative quantum sensing with shallow electron spins, such as those hosted by nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, requires accurate knowledge of the spin's depth below the host material's surface. A widely used approach infers this depth from the 1H nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) signal of immersion oil on the diamond surface that can be detected using dynamical decoupling sequences such as XY8. However, finite-width pulses make XY8 sensitive to subharmonic responses, including unwanted contributions from nearby 13C spins, and its instrument-limited spectral resolution provides only sparse sampling of the narrow 1H NMR lineshape. Here, we introduce Spin-Lock NMR as an alternative approach to single-NV depth determination. By tuning the Spin-Lock Rabi frequency to the 1H Larmor frequency, the NV probes the 1H NMR signal through the Hartmann-Hahn resonance without the harmonic ambiguities of pulsed decoupling sequences and with substantially higher instrument-limited spectral resolution. We derive a quantitative Spin-Lock NMR fit function from a Markovian master equation that directly relates the measured spectrum to the NV depth. Our approach yields NV depth estimates in excellent agreement with the established XY8-based protocol across multiple NV centers and establishes Spin-Lock NMR as a robust alternative for quantitative single-NV depth determination. To demonstrate its applicability, we employ our method to investigate the 1H nuclear spin signal that is regularly reported to be present on diamond, even in the absence of immersion oil.

</details>


### [120] [Impurity-induced Inverse Faraday Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17802)
*A. A. Kopasov,A. A. Bespalov,A. S. Mel'nikov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于量子力学描述，研究了在圆极化电磁波照射下，二维电子气中非磁性点杂质周围的光致直流电流态和磁场。通过含时薛定谔方程的二阶微扰解，发现垂直二维系统平面的直流磁场分量呈随机磁通分布，与标准逆法拉第效应（仅边缘电流）不同，空间平均直流磁场在远离样品边缘处不为零。该机制普遍适用于二维和三维系统，并能引起光致霍尔效应和法拉第旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于弛豫时间近似的流体力学或动力学方程方法只预测光致电流沿样品边缘流动，而本文旨在揭示无序系统中逆法拉第效应存在更丰富的体内机制——即杂质周围会形成整流电流环，产生内禀直流磁场，从而解释可能的光致霍尔效应和法拉第旋转。

Method: 使用含时薛定谔方程，在电磁波振幅的二阶微扰论框架下求解，对二维电子气中非磁性点杂质在圆极化光照射下的响应进行量子力学描述，得到光致直流电流和磁场的空间分布。

Result: 1) 垂直二维平面的直流磁场分量呈随机磁通分布，束缚在杂质位置附近；2) 空间平均直流磁场在远离样品边缘处不为零，与标准逆法拉第效应预测的边缘电流行为显著不同；3) 该机制具有普适性，适用于二维和三维系统。

Conclusion: 本文提出的杂质周围整流电流环形成机制是逆法拉第效应在无序系统中的新表现形式，产生的内禀直流磁场可导致光致霍尔效应和探测电磁信号的法拉第旋转，为理解光与无序物质相互作用提供了新的量子力学视角。

Abstract: We provide a quantum-mechanical description of the photoinduced dc current states and magnetic fields around nonmagnetic point impurities in a two-dimensional (2D) electron gas irradiated by a circularly polarized electromagnetic wave. Based on the solution of the corresponding time-dependent Schrodinger equation within the second-order perturbation theory in the electromagnetic wave amplitude we find that the resulting dc magnetic field component perpendicular to the plane of the 2D system is distributed like in a set of random magnetic fluxes bound to the positions of impurities. As a result, the spatially averaged dc magnetic field does not vanish far from the sample edges and, thus, our scenario of the inverse Faraday effect in disordered systems differs strongly from the standard one based on the relaxation time approximation within the hydrodynamic or kinetic equation approaches which would give only the photoinduced currents flowing along the sample edges. The predicted mechanism for formation of rectified currents flowing around the impurity centers is shown to be generic both for 2D and three-dimensional systems. The internal dc magnetic field can give rise to the photoinduced Hall effect and Faraday rotation for a probing electromagnetic signal.

</details>


### [121] [Physics-constrained machine learning for decoding multi-nanobubble configurations in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17866)
*Jihye Kim,Taegeun Song,Nojoon Myoung*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理约束的机器学习框架，通过解密度态谱来识别石墨烯纳米气泡的构型，利用多气泡DOS与单气泡谱加和关系的性质，实现可解释的构型解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电子谱中识别多个石墨烯纳米气泡具有挑战性，因为应变诱导的特征相互重叠。

Method: 开发了物理约束的机器学习框架，利用已验证的加和关系（多气泡DOS等于单气泡谱的归一化和），构建紧凑的神经分解模型，独立优化基系数以识别构型。

Result: 该方法能准确重构复杂度递增的构型，对重复组分、不完整基字典和模拟测量噪声均保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为表征应变工程石墨烯纳米结构提供了可解释的途径，并可推广至其他具有加和谱响应的量子材料。

Abstract: Identifying multiple graphene nanobubbles from electronic spectra is challenging because their strain-induced features overlap. We develop a physics-constrained machine-learning framework that decodes nanobubble configurations from density-of-states (DOS) spectra. For spatially separated nanobubbles, previous full quantum-transport calculations established that the multi-bubble DOS is numerically equivalent to the normalized sum of the constituent single-bubble spectra. We encode this validated additive relation in a compact neural decomposition model. For each target spectrum, the basis coefficients are optimized independently, and the resulting weights directly identify the constituent geometries. The method accurately reconstructs configurations of increasing complexity and remains robust to repeated constituents, incomplete basis dictionaries, and simulated measurement noise. The framework provides an interpretable route for characterizing strain-engineered graphene nanostructures and may extend to other quantum materials with additive spectral responses.

</details>


### [122] [Stripe-Order Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17997)
*Zheng-Yang Zhuang,Zhongbo Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了基于自旋反转镜像的条纹序交错磁性，区别于传统旋转对称性的l波分类，并揭示了条纹交错磁体与条纹反交错磁体两种相，后者可实现类铁电的自旋分裂电控，且在强跳跃各向异性下易于实现，提供了纯横向自旋电流的诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交错磁性机制主要依赖于Néel序反铁磁体中的自旋反转旋转对称性，缺乏基于镜像对称性的条纹序交错磁性的理解。

Method: 采用双轨道模型结合随机相位近似（RPA）计算，分析条纹自旋序与轨道序的相互作用。

Result: 发现了条纹交错磁体（镜面选择向列自旋分裂）和条纹反交错磁体（自旋简并能带）两种相；后者在适当翘曲结构中可支持类铁电的自旋分裂电控；强跳跃各向异性下近半填充时条纹序交错磁性更稳定；自旋反转镜像导致纯横向自旋电流。

Conclusion: 建立了基于镜像对称性的条纹序交错磁性新机制，拓展了交错磁性的分类体系，并提供了电控自旋分裂和纯横向自旋电流的探测手段。

Abstract: Altermagnetism combines compensated magnetic order with nonrelativistic spin splitting, yet established mechanisms predominantly rely on spin-reversing rotations in Néel-order antiferromagnets. Here we establish stripe-order altermagnetism governed instead by a spin-reversing mirror, placing it outside the usual rotation-based $l$-wave classification. Using two-orbital models, we show that the interplay of stripe spin and orbital orders yields two phases: a stripe altermagnet with mirror-selected nematic spin splitting and a stripe anti-altermagnet with spin-degenerate bands. The latter can support ferroelectric-like electrical control of spin splitting in suitable buckled structures. Random-phase-approximation (RPA) calculations show that stripe-order altermagnetism is favored near half filling under strong hopping anisotropy. The spin-reversing mirror further enforces a purely transverse spin current for an electric field parallel or normal to the mirror plane, providing a direct transport diagnostic.

</details>


### [123] [Contrasting $Γ$- and K-Valley Moiré Physics in Twisted Monolayer/Bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18212)
*Jackson Kuklin,Ning Mao,Milan Mandigo-Stoba,Edgar Elias,Tianci Song,Connor Engel,Pola Pietrzkowski,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Daniel Rhodes,Yang Zhang,Qianhui Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过扭角单层/双层WSe₂体系，利用位移场调控实现了K谷和Γ谷的莫尔物理，发现两谷轨道特性不同导致在填充因子ν=1和ν=1/3处呈现截然不同的关联相：K谷呈现弱绝缘体（ν=1）和广义Wigner晶体（ν=1/3），而Γ谷则表现出显著的Pomeranchuk效应，靠近Mott转变或结晶边界，表明轨道自由度在莫尔体系量子相中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维莫尔材料是探索关联现象的高度可调平台，但轨道自由度的作用尚不清楚。本文旨在研究不同轨道特征的谷（K谷与Γ谷）在莫尔体系中如何影响关联电子相，并探索Γ谷作为研究量子相变附近关联现象的新平台。

Method: 以扭角单层/双层WSe₂为体系，通过位移场调控使莫尔物理分别在K谷和Γ谷实现，对比两种谷在不同填充因子（ν=1和ν=1/3）下的输运和热力学行为。

Result: ν=1时，K谷态为弱绝缘体（类似扭角双层WSe₂的反铁磁态），Γ谷态表现出显著的Pomeranchuk效应（靠近Mott转变）。ν=1/3时，K谷为稳健的广义Wigner晶体，Γ谷靠近结晶边界，同样呈现Pomeranchuk效应，且升温和加磁场增强局域化。

Conclusion: 轨道特性在莫尔体系量子相的定义中至关重要，Γ谷是探索量子相变附近关联现象（竞争相与增强涨落可能产生非常规相）的有前景平台。

Abstract: Electronic orbital character plays a central role in determining electronic correlations, spin-orbit coupling, dimensionality, and ultimately the quantum phases of condensed-matter systems. Two-dimensional moiré materials have emerged as highly tunable platforms for exploring correlated phenomena, but the role of orbital degrees of freedom remains largely unexplored. Here, we identify twisted monolayer/bilayer WSe$_2$ as a platform in which displacement-field tuning enables moiré physics to be realized in both the $K$ and $Γ$ valleys. The distinct orbital characters of these valleys give rise to contrasting correlated phases at moiré filling factors $ν=1$ and $ν=1/3$. At $ν=1$, the $K$-valley state is a weak insulator, consistent with an antiferromagnetic state near a van Hove singularity in the intermediate-coupling regime, similar to that observed in twisted bilayer WSe$_2$. In contrast, the $Γ$-valley state exhibits a pronounced Pomeranchuk effect, consistent with proximity to a Mott transition. At $ν=1/3$, the $K$ valley hosts a robust generalized Wigner crystal, whereas the $Γ$-valley state lies near the crystallization boundary and again exhibits a Pomeranchuk effect, with localization enhanced by increasing temperature or magnetic field. Our work highlights the importance of orbital character in defining quantum phases in moiré systems, and identify the $Γ$ valley as a promising platform for exploring correlated phenomena near quantum phase transitions, where competing phases and enhanced fluctuations may give rise to unconventional phases.

</details>


### [124] [Position-dependent tight-binding model for Li impurities in monolayer and bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18214)
*Hernan Aguirre,Hernan L. Calvo,Eduardo M. Perassi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个半经验紧束缚模型，通过拟合DFT计算数据，研究了锂离子吸附于单层石墨烯和插层于双层石墨烯时的电子结构变化，发现锂杂质引入的扰动高度局域化，并揭示了单层与双层石墨烯中杂质势空间轮廓的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂吸附和插层会显著改变石墨烯基材料的低能电子性质，理解这些变化对于研究石墨电极中锂离子的输运和存储至关重要，因此需要一种高效、准确的模型来描述锂离子对石墨烯电子结构的影响。

Method: 发展了一个半经验紧束缚模型，将锂离子位置依赖性纳入其中；通过拟合不同构型、高度和超胞尺寸下的密度泛函理论计算结果来确定模型参数。

Result: 该模型精确复现了单层和双层石墨烯费米能级附近的电子能带；锂离子引入的扰动高度局域化，且随超胞尺寸增大而减弱；拟合参数揭示了单层与双层石墨烯中杂质势空间轮廓的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 所获模型为研究稀锂杂质提供了高效框架，并为未来研究石墨烯基材料中锂扩散及杂质诱导的输运现象奠定了有用基础。

Abstract: Lithium adsorption and intercalation can significantly modify the low-energy electronic properties of graphene-based materials, making their characterization relevant for understanding Li-ion transport and storage in graphitic electrodes. In this work, we investigate the electronic structure of a Li ion adsorbed on monolayer graphene (MLG) and intercalated within AB-stacked bilayer graphene (BLG). To this end, we develop a semi-empirical tight-binding model that incorporates Li-position dependence. Its parameters are determined by fitting to density-functional-theory calculations for different configurations, heights, and supercell sizes. The obtained model accurately reproduces the electronic bands near the Fermi level for both MLG and BLG and provides a transparent interpretation of the impurity-induced modifications in terms of symmetry breakings, intervalley mixing, and band-gap openings. We find that the perturbation introduced by the Li ion is strongly localized and that its effect decreases with increasing supercell size. The fitted parameters further reveal systematic differences between MLG and BLG in the spatial profile of the impurity potential. The obtained results provide an efficient framework for studying dilute Li impurities and constitute a useful starting point for future investigations of Li diffusion and impurity-induced transport phenomena in graphene-based materials.

</details>


### [125] [Non-Abelian Gauge Field Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18215)
*Ivan Velkovsky,Carlos Camacho,Tomoki Ozawa,Hannah Price,Bryce Gadway*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在主动机械晶格中实验实现了可调谐的非阿贝尔规范场，通过实时测量和反馈构建自旋相关耦合，并展示了非厄米非阿贝尔规范势下方向依赖的威尔逊环和局域化模式切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔规范场对于描述粒子运动与内部自由度（如自旋）耦合的行为至关重要，但实验实现可调谐的非阿贝尔规范场仍具挑战。

Method: 利用振荡器对编码局域赝自旋，通过实时测量和反馈构建自旋相关的位点间耦合，在主动机械晶格中实现可调谐的非阿贝尔规范场；通过威尔逊环观测验证非阿贝尔性；进一步引入非互易跳跃实现非厄米非阿贝尔规范势。

Result: 成功提取威尔逊环观测值，证明产生了真正的非阿贝尔规范场；在2D晶格中观察到方向依赖的威尔逊环；在1D系统中展示了非阿贝尔规范势可切换非厄米趋肤模的局域化端点。

Conclusion: 主动机械晶格是探索非阿贝尔规范场及其与非厄米动力学相互作用的灵活可编程平台。

Abstract: Non-Abelian gauge fields play a key role in describing the behavior of particles whose motion is coupled to internal degrees of freedom, such as their spin. Here, we experimentally realize a tuneable non-Abelian gauge field in an active mechanical lattice by using pairs of oscillators to encode a local pseudo-spin for each site, with inter-site spin-dependent couplings engineered via real-time measurement and feedback. We experimentally extract Wilson-loop observables in our set-up and hence demonstrate that we can create a genuinely non-Abelian gauge field. We then exploit the controllability of our mechanical lattice to engineer non-reciprocal hoppings to explore non-Hermitian non-Abelian gauge potentials. For a two-dimensional (2D) lattice, we demonstrate that the non-Hermiticity can manifest in direction-dependent Wilson loops for a single plaquette, while for a one-dimensional (1D) system, we show that a non-Abelian gauge potential can switch the localization of non-Hermitian skin modes between opposite ends of a chain. Our work establishes active mechanical lattices as a flexible and programmable platform for probing non-Abelian gauge fields and exploring their interplay with non-Hermitian dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

### [126] [Two-dimensional solitons in extended GPE models with Lee-Huang-Yang corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16820)
*G. N. Koutsokostas,F. Bristy,E. C. Psychogiou,G. A. Bougas,G. C. Katsimiga,S. I. Mistakidis,P. G. Kevrekidis,D. J. Frantzeskakis*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子液滴环境中由扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程描述的两维孤立波的存在性与动力学，通过多尺度渐近方法推导出可积非线性模型，构造了线孤子、团块孤子、环孤子和dromion等近似解析解，并通过数值模拟验证了它们的动力学鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子液滴环境中两维孤立波的存在性与动力学行为，揭示平均场与量子涨落竞争下的新型多维孤子解，为当前超冷原子实验提供理论依据。

Method: 采用多尺度渐近方法，从包含对数平均场和Lee-Huang-Yang相互作用的扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发，在背景调制稳定区域推导出Kadomtsev-Petviashvili和Davey-Stewartson等有效可积非线性模型，进而构造近似解析解，并通过数值模拟监测动力学鲁棒性。

Result: 成功构造了线孤子、代数局域团块孤子、环孤子和指数局域dromion四种多维孤子解；线孤子、团块孤子和环孤子与理论预测最为接近，仅因小振幅辐射产生渐进偏离；dromion偏离解析波形最大，但大致保持形状。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了平均场与量子涨落竞争模型中前所未有的多维孤子解，这些解在实验上可通过超冷原子系统实现。

Abstract: We investigate the existence and dynamics of two-dimensional solitary waves in a quantum droplet environment described by the extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation featuring logarithmic mean-field and Lee-Huang-Yang interactions. In the modulationally stable regime of the background, we employ suitable multiscale asymptotic methods to derive effective nonlinear integrable models corresponding to the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili and Davey-Stewartson equations. Based on these reduced models, we construct approximate analytical solutions describing line solitons, algebraically localized lump solitons, ring solitons, and exponentially localized dromions embedded on the droplet background. The dynamical robustness of these solutions is monitored through numerical simulations. Line, lump and ring solitons stay closest to the theoretical predictions, although progressively deviate due to the emergence of small-amplitude radiation, while dromions depart from their analytical waveform the most, although they roughly maintain their shape. Our results unveil unprecedented multidimensional soliton solutions in models featuring the competition of mean-field and quantum fluctuations and as such are amenable to current ultracold atom experiments.

</details>


### [127] [Coexistence of long- and quasi-long range spatial order in 1D quantum quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17334)
*A. Mendoza-Coto,M. Grossklags,J. Stefaniak,T. Donner,F. Piazza*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 准晶中一种独特的Goldstone模间隙机制：仅使与一个波矢相关的Goldstone模产生能隙，而另一个保持无隙，导致一维系统中出现有隙波矢的长程有序和无隙波矢的准长程有序共存现象，并提出了利用超冷玻色原子在光学腔中实现该方案的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索准晶中特有的、仅使一个Goldstone模产生能隙而另一个保持无隙的物理机制，揭示准晶中长程有序与准长程有序共存的独特现象。

Method: 理论分析准晶中两个不可公度波矢对应的平移对称性自发破缺产生的两个Goldstone模（声子和相位子），证明存在一种仅使其中一个波矢对应的Goldstone模产生能隙的机制，并提出了利用超冷玻色原子在光学腔中实现该物理情景的实验方案。

Result: 发现准晶中一种独特的Goldstone模间隙机制：仅使与一个波矢相关的Goldstone模产生能隙，而另一个保持无隙；在一维系统中，这导致有隙波矢方向呈现长程有序，无隙波矢方向因持续涨落仅呈现准长程有序。

Conclusion: 准晶中存在一种独有的、选择性使Goldstone模产生能隙的机制，导致不同波矢方向的有序性质出现差异，并可通过超冷玻色原子在光学腔中的实验体系实现该现象。

Abstract: Quasicrystals exhibit long-range positional order without periodicity, arising from multiple incommensurate wave vectors. In self-assembled quasicrystals, the spontaneous breaking of translational invariance gives rise to two Goldstone modes-phonons and phasons-associated with two competing wave vectors. Here, we demonstrate a unique scenario exclusive to quasicrystals: a mechanism that gaps out only one Goldstone mode (associated with one wave vector), while the other remains gapless. In one dimension, this leads to the coexistence of long-range order at the gapped wave vector and quasi-long-range order at the gapless one, due to sustained fluctuations. We show that this phenomenon can be realized using ultracold bosonic atoms in optical cavities.

</details>

### [128] [Gaussian Reformulation of the Feynman Path Integral for Quantum Statistical Mechanics with Results for the Second Virial Coefficient of $^4$He](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16301)
*Phil Attard*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将费曼路径积分重新表述为每个位置构型邻域的高斯采样，通过晶格上环聚合物统计获得方差，通过Wigner-Kirkwood对易函数的高温展开获得均值。该方法避免了传统路径积分量子蒙特卡洛中每个构型需要多个温度节点以及统计平均中的数值抵消问题。氦气第二维里系数的解析和模拟结果与实验室测量值进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统路径积分量子蒙特卡洛方法中存在的两个主要问题：每个位形构型需要多个温度节点，以及统计平均中需要数值抵消带来的计算困难。

Method: 将费曼路径积分重新表述为位置构型邻域的高斯采样，利用晶格上环聚合物统计计算方差，利用Wigner-Kirkwood对易函数的高温展开计算均值。

Result: 氦气第二维里系数的解析计算结果和模拟结果均与实验室测量值吻合良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的高斯采样路径积分方法能够有效规避传统路径积分量子蒙特卡洛的数值问题，并为量子统计力学的计算提供了一种更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: The Feynman path integral for quantum statistical mechanics is reformulated as Gaussian sampling of the neighborhood of each position configuration. The variance and mean are obtained from ring polymer statistics on a lattice, and from the high temperature expansion of the Wigner-Kirkwood commutation function, respectively. The algorithm avoids multiple temperature nodes for each configuration and the need for numerical cancelation in the statistical averages, which are problematic for conventional path integral quantum Monte Carlo. Analytic and simulation results for the second virial coefficient of helium are compared to laboratory measurements.

</details>


### [129] [Representation-Dependent Machine Learning of the Isotropic-Nematic Transition in the Lebwohl-Lasher Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16481)
*Maninder Kaur,Aojie Xue,David P Landau*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了无监督机器学习检测相变时，输入表示的对称性至关重要。对于Lebwohl-Lasher模型的弱一级各向同性-向列相变，PCA和卷积自编码器在原始分子构型上失败，但在旋转不变局域关联表示下成功检测到相变；而监督CNN在有标签时能从原始构型准确预测序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无监督机器学习检测相变时，输入表示是否保留系统对称性对检测效果的影响，特别是针对具有无极性连续简并向列相的三维Lebwohl-Lasher模型。

Method: 对比无监督方法（PCA和卷积自编码器CAE）与监督方法（三维卷积神经网络CNN）在原始分子构型和旋转不变局域关联表示两种输入表示下，对弱一级各向同性-向列相变的检测效果。

Result: 无监督方法在原始构型上失败，因为旋转等效的向列相在输入空间中相距甚远；但在旋转不变表示下成功检测到相变敏感特征和双峰共存分布。监督CNN在有序参数标签下能从原始构型准确预测标量序参量。

Conclusion: Lebwohl-Lasher模型区分了无监督相发现与监督序参量回归，表明在取向有序系统中，无监督机器学习需要尊重对称性的输入表示。

Abstract: Machine-learning detection of phase transitions depends not only on the learning algorithm, but also on whether the input representation preserves the symmetries of the system. We examine this for the weak first-order isotropic--nematic transition of the three-dimensional Lebwohl--Lasher model, whose apolar and continuously degenerate nematic phase makes raw molecular configurations a challenging input for unsupervised learning. Principal component analysis (PCA) and a convolutional autoencoder (CAE) fail to identify the transition from raw configurations because rotationally equivalent nematic states can appear far apart in the input space. When the same configurations are transformed into a rotationally invariant local-correlation representation, both methods recover transition-sensitive signatures and bimodal coexistence distributions. A supervised three-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN), by contrast, accurately predicts the scalar order parameter from raw configurations when given order-parameter labels. The Lebwohl-Lasher model therefore separates unsupervised phase discovery from supervised order-parameter regression and shows that symmetry-respecting input representations are needed for unsupervised machine learning in orientationally ordered systems.

</details>


### [130] [Glauber dynamics phase transitions in athermal random field Blume-Capel and Blume-Emery-Grifitths models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16561)
*Sumedha,Aldrin B E*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了完全图上高斯随机场中Glauber动力学与非平衡稳态的解析解，发现了随机场方差R的临界值R_c，比较了平衡态与非平衡稳态，分析了外磁场下的滞后现象，并推导了磁滞回线的形状与矫顽场方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究完全图上的Glauber动力学稳态与非平衡稳态的关系，探究随机场方差R对系统行为的影响，以及外磁场下磁滞回线的形状与相变特性。

Method: 通过解析求解完全图上的Glauber动力学模型，比较平衡态与非平衡稳态，引入Blume-Emery-Griffiths模型的排斥双二次相互作用，分析连续相变与一级相变的位置，推导磁滞回线的形状与矫顽场方程。

Result: 低R值时Glauber动力学稳态依赖于初始状态，超过临界值R_c后平衡态与非平衡稳态重合；R类似温度的作用。排斥双二次相互作用可使R_c变为零。准静态演化中系统可从R_c=0交叉到随机场Ising模型普适类（R_c=√(2/π)）。外磁场下展示矩形、六边形、平行四边形、细腰型和双磁滞回线。磁滞回线面积由R决定，形状由R=0行为决定。

Conclusion: 完全图上高斯随机场中的Glauber动力学具有丰富的非平衡稳态行为，R_c是关键参数，系统可表现出多种磁滞回线形状，相图依赖于初始状态。

Abstract: We solve the two models for Glauber dynamics and in equilibrium, both in the presence and absence of the external magnetic field on a complete graph. We compare the steady state of the Glauber dynamics with equilibrium and find that for low values of variance $R$ of the Gaussian random field, the steady state of the Glauber dynamics depends on the initial state. Beyond a critical value $R_{c}$ the equilibrium and non equilibrium steady states coincide. The variance $R$ in random field models behaves similar to the temperature. The location of both the continuous and first order transitions can be obtained exactly for the Glauber dynamics steady state. The frustration is introduced by considering repulsive bi-quadratic interaction for Blume-Emery-Griffiths model. We also. consider repulsive bi-quadratic interaction and show that $R_c$ can become zero depending on the value of the crystal field. Interestingly, we also find that even when a system has $R_c=0$ at the start of quasi-static evolution with Glauber dynamics, with increasing $R$, in some regime of the couplings, the model undergoes a crossover to a random field Ising model universaility with $R_c$ changing from $0$ to $\sqrt{\frac{2}π}$. In the presence of uniform magnetic field, regions of first order transition exhibit hysteresis under Glauber dynamics. These models exhibit rectangular, hexagonal, parallelogram, wasp-waisted, and double hysteresis loops. We derive the shapes of hysteresis loops analytically giving the equation for the value of the coercive field and show that while the area under the hysteresis loop depends on $R$, the shape is determined by the behavior of the models at $R=0$. In particular, in the case of Blume-Emery-Griffiths model the hysteresis plots have regions of continuous and first order transitions both, resulting in a rich phase diagram that depends non-trivially on the initial state.

</details>


### [131] [On the Information Required for Feedback Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16639)
*Matthew P. Leighton,Jose M. Betancourt,Thierry Emonet,Benjamin B. Machta,Michael C. Abbott*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机系统在有限信息约束下的反馈控制问题，推导了从受控系统到控制器所需的信息率下界，并建立了性能-信息帕累托前沿。对于状态无关被动动力学的系统，该下界可通过一种概率性时间反演被动动力学的最优控制协议达到，并在非线性粒子定位、微生物导航和信息引擎等应用中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统与工程问题中常需在有限信息条件下控制噪声系统，现有理论缺乏对信息率下界及性能-信息权衡的通用框架。

Method: 推导信息率下界，提出概率性时间反演被动动力学的最优控制协议，并通过非线性粒子定位、微生物导航和信息引擎实验验证。

Result: 得到了信息率下界及性能-信息帕累托前沿；对于状态无关被动动力学系统，该下界可被显式最优控制协议饱和。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解生物系统与工程中信息受限下的控制权衡提供了理论基础，且所提出的最优控制协议具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Biological systems across scales, along with many engineering problems, must control noisy systems with limited information. Here we study information-limited feedback control of stochastic systems to achieve target steady states, and derive a lower bound on the information rate from controlled system to controller. This framework allows us to obtain performance-information Pareto frontiers for wide-ranging control problems with limited information. For systems with state-independent passive dynamics, the bound is saturated by an explicit optimal control protocol which probabilistically time-reverses the passive dynamics. We showcase these results through applications to nonlinear particle localization, microbial navigation, and experimentally realized information engines.

</details>


### [132] [Occupation-condensation transition of a sublinearly vertex-reinforced random walk on regular tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16971)
*Bon A Koo,Edward Ju*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了顶点强化随机游走在b叉树上的行为，发现当记忆强度参数β超过临界值β_c时，会出现占据测度的凝聚相变：低于临界值时游走范围线性增长，高于临界值时单个顶点占据O(1)比例的时间，而游走范围仍缓慢增长（约log t）。四个估计量定位到相同阈值，且可逆性分析预测β_c ∝ b-1。临界点附近占据数非自平均且呈双峰分布，呈现共存现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解顶点强化随机游走在指数增长树结构上的行为，探究记忆效应与树结构指数增长之间的竞争如何导致占据测度的凝聚相变，以及该相变与有限范围局域化之间的区别。

Method: 在b叉树上定义顶点强化随机游走（步进概率正比于1+βn^a），通过数值模拟（t=3×10^7）和四种估计量定位相变阈值；利用冻结环境下的可逆性分析，将游走转化为边电导c_{uv}=w_u w_v的模型，结合分支数准则预测β_c；通过b=2,3,4的模拟数据验证β_c ∝ b-1的标度关系。

Result: 发现了尖锐的凝聚相变：低于β_c时占据扩散且范围线性增长；高于β_c时单个顶点凝聚O(1)的时间分数，范围约按log t增长而非幂律；四个估计量给出相同阈值且无系统漂移；b=2,3,4的β_c除以b-1后数据坍塌至百分之几精度；临界点处占据数呈双峰分布。

Conclusion: 证实了树结构上顶点强化随机游走存在与有限范围局域化不同的凝聚相变；可逆性分析成功预测了相变阈值与分支数的关系；揭示了临界点的非自平均和双峰共存现象，为理解强化随机过程在非欧几何上的行为提供了新见解。

Abstract: A vertex-reinforced random walk steps to a neighbour with probability proportional to $1+βn^{a}$, where $n$ counts previous visits to that neighbour and $a\in(0,1)$ sets the memory strength. On the rooted $b$-ary tree the exponential growth of the vertex set drives the walk outward while the reinforcement pulls it back. We report a sharp condensation transition of the occupation measure at a finite $β_c(a,b)$: below it the occupation spreads and the range grows linearly; above it a single vertex holds an $O(1)$ fraction of the time, stable in the observation time, while the range keeps growing very slowly, at a rate better described by $\log t$ than by any power. We do not find the range to be bounded, and keep this condensation distinct from finite-range localization. Four estimators locate the same threshold, which shows no systematic drift out to $t=3\times10^{7}$. In a frozen environment the walk is reversible, with edge conductances $c_{uv}=w_{u}w_{v}$, $w_{v}=1+βn_{v}^{a}$, and measure $μ_{v}\propto w_{v}\sum_{u\sim v}w_{u}$ describing the condensed core, whose neighbour coupling we test directly. Reversibility places the escape at the frontier within the branching-number criterion for biased walks on trees, predicting $β_c\propto b-1$; the measured lines for $b=2,3,4$ collapse under division by $b-1$ to a few percent (bootstrap). The value $a=1/2$ that governs the walk on $\mathbb{Z}$ enters only as the marginal exponent of the condensed profile. Near $β_c$ the occupancy is non-self-averaging and bimodal, a coexistence-type phenomenology.

</details>


### [133] [Floquet Driving of Enzymatic Reactions: Counting Statistics and Long-Time Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17072)
*Yuki Watanabe,Yuki Ishiguro,Takashi Oka*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发展了经典随机过程的Floquet理论，用于计算周期性调制下化学反应的流及其计数统计。通过计数场形式，推导了第一累积量和电流的通用表达式，包括高频和低频渐近行为。以离散Floquet驱动的Michaelis-Menten酶促反应（cAMP生产）为例，展示了周期性开关催化活性如何产生长时产物电流，并揭示了高频极限下周期驱动可等效为有效反应速率修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着光遗传学等人工控制化学反应系统技术的快速发展，理解时间依赖控制下的反应动力学变得日益重要。当反应速率的调制呈周期性时，需要建立系统性的理论框架来分析此类问题。

Method: 基于Floquet形式论，将计数场引入经典随机过程，推导了周期性调制下电流和计数统计的通用表达式。利用有效Floquet生成元和kicked态表示电流，并给出高/低频极限下的渐近表达式。通过离散Floquet驱动（阶跃式开/关催化活性）的Michaelis-Menten模型进行非微扰验证。

Result: 得到了周期性驱动下电流的第一累积量通用表达式；在高频极限下，周期驱动可等效为化学反应速率的有效修正；在离散驱动模型中，解析和数值结果均表明周期性开关催化活性会产生长时产物电流。

Conclusion: 该工作为周期性驱动化学反应系统的Floquet分析提供了理论基础，揭示了周期调制可等效为有效反应速率修正的物理图景。

Abstract: Technologies for artificially controlling chemical reaction systems, such as optogenetics, are rapidly advancing, making it increasingly important to understand reaction dynamics under time-dependent control. When the modulation of reaction rates is periodic in time, the Floquet formalism provides a systematic framework. We develop a Floquet theory for classical stochastic processes that enables the calculation of the current and its counting statistics under such periodic modulation. In particular, we formulate the theory in terms of a counting field and derive general expressions for the first cumulant and the corresponding current. The current is expressed using the effective Floquet generator and the kicked state, and we further obtain general asymptotic expressions for the current in both the high- and low-frequency regimes. As a concrete example to test our analytical expressions, we then apply the results to discrete Floquet driving -- a non-perturbative, stepwise protocol. The setup is motivated by a biochemical system known as cyclic adenosine monophosphate (cAMP) production, which is an enzymatic reaction activated and inhibited by G-proteins. This is formulated as a discretely driven Michaelis--Menten-type reaction model, in which the catalytic activity is switched on and off abruptly in time, and we obtain analytical expressions and numerical results showing how periodic switching of reaction rates generates a long-time product current. In particular, in the high-frequency limit, we show that the effect of the periodic driving can be interpreted through an effective modification of the chemical reaction rates. These results provide a basis for Floquet analysis of periodically driven chemical reactions.

</details>


### [134] [Adverse Selection with Quality Variance: A Maximum-Entropy Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17239)
*Zhi-Lei Zhang,Tan-Ji Zhou,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出将逆向选择建模为质量分布的动态截断过程，揭示了市场不完全退化的机制，并分析了质量方差、价格溢价等因素对市场退化速度及最终稳定质量平台的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆向选择模型中仅用均值质量描述市场，无法解释市场退化速度及质量分布演化过程。本文旨在通过统计方法解决这两个问题。

Method: 将逆向选择建模为质量分布的动态截断过程：买家设定一个与均值质量成比例（比例因子ξ>1）的上界，超出该上界的卖家退出市场；保留市场由截断后的条件分布刻画，迭代该过程直至市场质量达到稳定状态。

Result: （i）识别了防止完全逆向选择的机制；（ii）更大的质量方差或更小的价格溢价会提高均值质量退化的上界；（iii）最大熵基准数值实验显示质量方差与支付率共同决定市场退化程度及最终稳定质量平台。

Conclusion: 该统计方法阐明了市场干预如何减缓逆向选择：提高买家支付率、降低质量方差或提高最低质量下限。

Abstract: The adverse-selection mechanism in markets explains how asymmetric information between buyers and sellers can drive high-quality goods out of the market, thereby causing market deterioration. In its simplest formulation, only the mean quality is used to describe the market, and this is insufficient to determine how fast the market deteriorates or how the quality distribution evolves. To resolve the two problems, we describe the adverse selection as a dynamic truncation of the quality distribution: buyers set an upper bound proportional to the mean quality by a rate $ξ$ that is larger than unity, and sellers whose quality exceeds this upper bound reject an offer and exit the market. The retained market is then characterized by the conditional distribution obtained after this truncation, and the corresponding evolution process is iterated until market quality reaches a stable state. This statistical approach gives three results. (i) We identify a mechanism for preventing complete adverse selection, defined as the process where the quality of the market is driven down to the minimum quality floor. (ii) A larger quality variance or a smaller price premium, defined as the amount by which the payment upper bound exceeds the current mean quality, raises the upper bound on the deterioration in mean quality. (iii) A maximum-entropy benchmark shows numerically how quality variance and the payment rate jointly determine market deterioration and the final stable quality platform. This approach also clarifies how market interventions can slow adverse selection: they may raise buyers' payment rate, reduce quality variance, or increase the minimum quality floor.

</details>


### [135] [Richards' equation as a hydrodynamic limit: Chapman--Enskog reduction of the continuum kinetic equation for unsaturated soil water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17358)
*Riccardo Rigon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从孔隙填充分布函数的动力学理论出发，推导了非饱和水流运动的Richards方程，并通过Chapman-Enskog约化揭示了从微观动力学到宏观Richards方程的多尺度渐近联系，建立了滞留曲线、水力传导率等经典概念的微观基础，并首次从第一性原理推导了双重/多重渗透率模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宏观理论将空间和时间的极限混为一谈，缺乏从微观孔隙尺度动力学到宏观Richards方程的严格渐近推导。本文旨在通过Chapman-Enskog约化，分离时空极限，建立孔隙填充分布动力学与非饱和水流宏观方程之间的理论基础。

Method: 采用Chapman-Enskog约化方法，以Damköhler数（Da，即重新分布时间与外力时间之比）为控制参数，对封闭连续动力学方程（CKE）进行多尺度展开。利用线性化重新分布算子的自伴性和负半定性，在质量内积下进行谱分析，逐级求解CE层级方程。

Result: 1) 平衡阶定义了滞留曲线；2) 一阶方程给出了Richards方程，质量守恒作为可解性条件；3) 响应函数给出了宏观通量，水力传导率K作为一阶输运系数（类似粘度的对应物）；4) 平均场约化恢复了标准积分公式，串联路径修正给出了Mualem异质性惩罚；5) 双峰孔隙分布打开了松弛谱间隙，通过带内CE约化与带间松弛耦合，从第一性原理推导了双重/多重渗透率模型，交换系数由带间连通性决定。

Conclusion: Richards方程是CKE在小Damköhler数极限下的一阶Chapman-Enskog近似；当Da不小即非平衡效应显著时，展开失效，需直接求解完整的CKE。该框架为多尺度非饱和水流理论提供了严格的微观基础，并统一了不同唯象模型。

Abstract: Richards' equation for unsaturated water flow is derived from the kinetic theory of the pore-filling distribution g(r,x,t) of a companion paper. It separates two limits that macroscopic theory usually conflates. The spatial limit is purely kinematic: contracting the representative elementary volume (REV) to a point yields the closed continuum kinetic equation (CKE) d_t g + div F = C[g], with F a pore-resolved pre-closure flux and C[g] the occupancy-gated redistribution operator. The dynamics lies in the temporal limit, the subject of this paper: a Chapman-Enskog (CE) reduction of the CKE controlled by the Damkohler number Da (redistribution time over forcing time), the structural analogue of the passage from Boltzmann to Navier-Stokes. The linearized redistribution operator J is self-adjoint and negative semidefinite in the mass inner product, with a one-dimensional kernel fixing a single invariant (water) and hence one macroscopic equation; the CE hierarchy inverts the same J at every order, only the source changing. Four results follow: the equilibrium step defines the retention curve; the linearized water budget plus the inter-REV source set the first-order equation; its solvability is mass conservation, i.e. Richards' equation; and the response function gives the macroscopic flux, identifying the conductivity K as a first-order transport coefficient, the counterpart of viscosity. Mean-field reduction recovers the standard integral formula; the serial-path correction gives Mualem's heterogeneity penalty. A bimodal pore-size distribution opens a gap in the relaxation spectrum; projecting onto the bands and applying CE within each, with cross-band relaxation surviving at O(1), derives the dual- and multiple-permeability models from first principles, the exchange coefficient set by inter-band connectivity. When Da is not small the expansion breaks down and the full CKE is needed.

</details>


### [136] [On the use of the Belopol'skaya-Daletskii representation of a diffusion on a Riemann manifold to construct path integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17871)
*Paolo Muratore-Ginanneschi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文展示了Belopol'skaya-Daletskii框架如何通过指数映射描述扩散增量，从而为扩散路径测度的有限维近似构造等变表示提供了一种基本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为黎曼流形上的扩散过程路径测度的有限维近似构造等变表示，解决传统方法在几何结构利用上的不足。

Method: 采用Belopol'skaya-Daletskii的随机微分方程公式化方法，利用指数映射描述扩散的增量，从而构造等变表示。

Result: 成功构造了扩散路径测度有限维近似的等变表示，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Belopol'skaya-Daletskii框架提供了一种简洁有效的途径来实现扩散路径测度的等变表示，指数映射是关键工具。

Abstract: We show that the Belopol'skaya-Daletskii formulation of stochastic differential equations on a Riemann manifold offers an elementary way to construct equivariant representations of finite-dimensional approximations to the path measure of a diffusion. The key ingredient is the use of the exponential map to describe increments of the diffusion.

</details>


### [137] [A Unified Discrete and Continuous Theory of Core-Halo Complexity Maximizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17907)
*Akshat Sharma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对离散和连续概率空间，建立了广义统计复杂度的变分框架，推导出统一的驻点方程，证明所有驻解具有恰好两个概率水平（核心-晕结构），并证明全局复杂度最大值由最小的可允许核心实现（离散中为单一主导态，连续中为无穷小核心）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明复杂度最大化的分布在有限离散系统中呈现两水平结构，但离散与连续概率空间缺乏统一的处理框架。

Method: 基于Shannon熵和Rényi熵构造广义统计复杂度，建立变分优化框架，推导统一的驻点方程，通过分析证明解的普遍结构。

Result: 证明了驻点解必然具有恰好两个概率水平（核心-晕结构）；优化问题约化为单一重数参数；全局复杂度最大值由最小可允许核心实现。

Conclusion: 该框架为离散和连续情形下的统计复杂度优化提供了统一的数学基础，可应用于统计力学、信息论和复杂系统分析。

Abstract: The maximization of statistical complexity has long been associated with the emergence of probability distributions lying between perfect order and complete disorder. While previous studies have shown that complexity-maximizing distributions exhibit a two-level structure in finite discrete systems, an analogous unified treatment for both discrete and continuous probability spaces has remained unavailable. In this work, we develop a general variational framework for a generalized statistical complexity constructed from Shannon and Renyi entropies. We derive a common stationary equation governing both discrete probability masses and continuous probability densities and prove that every stationary solution necessarily possesses exactly two probability levels, establishing a universal core-halo structure. We further demonstrate that the optimization problem reduces to a single multiplicity parameter and prove that the global complexity maximum is attained by the smallest admissible core, corresponding to a single dominant state in the discrete case and an infinitesimal core in the continuous limit. These results provide a complete analytical characterization of the complexity-maximizing distributions and reveal a common mathematical structure underlying complexity optimization in both discrete and continuous settings. The framework establishes a unified foundation for generalized statistical complexity with potential applications in statistical mechanics, information theory, and the analysis of complex systems.

</details>


### [138] [Large scale behavior in the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation: The Schwinger-Dyson route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17915)
*Olivier Coquand*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用Schwinger-Dyson框架研究Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程的大尺度性质，证明在红外极限下能维持稳定标度性的唯一解具有负有效粘度，且该性质不依赖于重整化群流截断或数值方案的特定选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在严格证明Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程在红外极限下稳定标度解的必要条件是负有效粘度，并表明这是波动方程的普遍性质，而非特定数值或理论截断的人为产物。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Dyson框架，在最小化假设条件下进行分析，避免了重整化群流截断或特定数值方案的局限性。

Result: 证明了唯一能维持红外稳定标度性的解具有负有效粘度，且该结论具有一般性。

Conclusion: 负有效粘度是Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程在红外极限下稳定标度解的固有性质，不依赖于具体计算方法的限制。

Abstract: The present paper is a study of the large scale properties of the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation. By using a Schwinger-Dyson framework, we aim to provide a proof that the only solutions that can sustain a stable scaling in the infrared limit have a negative effective viscosity (in the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky sense) with a minimal set of hypotheses, thereby showing that this constitutes a general property of the wave equation that does not depend on a specific set of truncations of a renormalisation group flow, or limitations of a given numerical scheme for example.

</details>


### [139] [Expansion of a free Fermi gas released from an isotropic trapping potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18007)
*J M Luck,P L Krapivsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了非相互作用费米子在任意维度各向同性陷阱势阱中制备的多体基态，在突然释放势阱后费米子云的弹道膨胀行为，利用半经典技术推导了大粒子数条件下的晚时间分布轮廓，并给出了任意幂指数a和维度d下的显式表达式，发现了动量分布和空间分布的普适边缘指数d/a，推广了维格纳半圆律和托马斯-费米分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解冷原子云在势阱突然释放后的膨胀动力学，特别是费米子系统的普适膨胀行为及其与维度、势阱指数之间的关系。

Method: 采用半经典（semi-classical）技术，在大粒子数近似下分析非相互作用费米子系统在弹道膨胀后的晚期分布轮廓。

Result: 推导出了任意幂指数a和任意维度d下膨胀云的空间分布和动量分布的显式表达式，发现边缘行为由普适指数d/a刻画。

Conclusion: 费米子在弹道膨胀后的边缘行为具有普适性，该结果将维格纳半圆律和托马斯-费米分布推广到了更一般的势阱和维度情形。

Abstract: We consider a system of non-interacting fermions prepared in the many-body ground state of an isotropic trapping potential in any dimension, and investigate the ballistic expansion of the fermionic cloud after the potential is suddenly released. Using semi-classical techniques, we derive the full late-time profile of the expanding cloud in the regime when the fermion number is large. We thus obtain explicit expressions for power-law potentials with arbitrary exponent $a$ and in all dimensions $d$. The momentum distribution and the spatial profile of the cloud exhibit a universal edge exponent $d/a$, thus generalizing the Wigner semi-circle law and the Thomas-Fermi distribution.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>
